===== app/types/terms.ts =====
export type TermType =
  | 'TERMS_OF_USE'
  | 'PRIVACY_POLICY'
  | 'COOKIES_POLICY'
  | 'PROCESSING_OPERATIONS';

export type TermStatus = 'ARCHIVED' | 'PUBLISHED' | 'DRAFT' | 'IN_REVIEW' | 'APPROVED';

export interface TermResponseDTO {
  id: number;
  version: string;
  content: string; // Markdown
  type: TermType;
  status: TermStatus;
  timestamp: string; // Instant, ISO-8601
}

export interface AcceptTermRequest {
  email: string;
  type: TermType;
}

export const termTypes: TermType[] = [
  'TERMS_OF_USE',
  'PRIVACY_POLICY',
  'COOKIES_POLICY',
  'PROCESSING_OPERATIONS'
];

export const termTypeLabels: Record<TermType, string> = {
  TERMS_OF_USE: 'Termos de Uso',
  PRIVACY_POLICY: 'Política de Privacidade',
  COOKIES_POLICY: 'Política de Cookies',
  PROCESSING_OPERATIONS: 'Relatório de Operações'
};

export const termStatusLabels: Record<TermStatus, string> = {
  ARCHIVED: 'Arquivado',
  PUBLISHED: 'Publicado',
  DRAFT: 'Rascunho',
  IN_REVIEW: 'Em revisão',
  APPROVED: 'Aprovado',
};

export function formatTermTimestamp(timestamp: string): string {
  const date = new Date(timestamp);

  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
    return timestamp;
  }

  return date.toLocaleString('pt-BR', {
    day: '2-digit',
    month: '2-digit',
    year: 'numeric',
    hour: '2-digit',
    minute: '2-digit',
  });
}

===== app/types/user.ts =====
import type { TermType } from "./terms";

export type UserRole = "admin" | "manager" | "teacher" | "student"; // lowercase = URL path segments
export type ApiRole = "ADMIN" | "MANAGER" | "TEACHER" | "STUDENT"; // uppercase = API responses

export interface User {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  email: string;
  institutionId: string | null;
  role: ApiRole;
}

export interface CreateUserRequest {
  name: string;
  email: string;
  institutionId?: string;
}

export interface CreateInstitutionalUserRequest {
  name: string;
  email: string;
  role: "manager" | "teacher" | "student";
  institutionId?: string;
}

export interface UpdateUserRequest {
  name?: string;
  email?: string;
  institutionId?: string | null;
}

export interface CompleteRegistrationRequest {
  password: string;
  code: string;
  email: string;
  acceptedTerms: TermType[];
}

export interface PendingUser {
  // 202 response
  name: string;
  email: string;
  role: ApiRole;
  institutionId: string | null;
}

export interface Page<T> {
  content: T[];
  totalElements: number;
  totalPages: number;
  number: number;
  size: number;
  first: boolean;
  last: boolean;
  empty: boolean;
}

export const userRoles = ["admin", "manager", "teacher", "student"] as const;

export const userRoleLabels: Record<UserRole, string> = {
  admin: "Administrador",
  manager: "Gestor",
  teacher: "Professor",
  student: "Estudante",
};

const apiRoleToUserRole: Record<ApiRole, UserRole> = {
  ADMIN: "admin",
  MANAGER: "manager",
  TEACHER: "teacher",
  STUDENT: "student",
};

export function roleToUserRole(apiRole: ApiRole): UserRole {
  return apiRoleToUserRole[apiRole];
}

===== app/types/dashboard.ts =====
export interface InstitutionDevices {
  institutionId: string;
  institutionName: string;
  institutionActive: boolean;
  active: number;
  inactive: number;
  operating: number;
}

export interface DevicesSummary {
  pending: number;
  active: number;
  inactive: number;
  operating: number;
  blockedByInstitution: number;
  institutions: InstitutionDevices[];
}

export interface DailyUsage {
  date: string; // yyyy-MM-dd
  connections: number;
  plays: number;
  pauses: number;
  stops: number;
}

export interface UsageSummary {
  from: string;
  to: string;
  connections: number;
  plays: number;
  pauses: number;
  stops: number;
  previousPlays: number;
  activeDevices: number;
  days: DailyUsage[];
}

export interface ScenarioUsage {
  scenarioId: string;
  name: string;
  plays: number;
  devices: number;
}

export interface DeviceUsage {
  deviceId: string;
  name: string;
  plays: number;
  lastEventAt: string | null;
}

export interface Rankings {
  scenarios: ScenarioUsage[];
  devices: DeviceUsage[];
}

export const dashboardPeriods = [7, 30, 90] as const;
export type DashboardPeriod = (typeof dashboardPeriods)[number];

===== app/components/organisms/AnimalAddModal.tsx =====
import { useState } from "react";

import type { Animal, AnimalDataRequest } from "~/types/Animal";
import { ModalLongTextField } from "../atoms/modal/ModalLongTextField";
import { ModalTextField } from "../atoms/modal/ModalTextField";
import { ModalTitle } from "../atoms/modal/ModalTitle";
import { ModalFormButtons } from "../molecules/ModalFormButtons";
import { Modal } from "../atoms/modal/Modal";
import { ModalForm } from "../atoms/modal/ModalForm";

interface AnimalAddModalProps {
  onClose: () => void;
  onSubmit: (data: AnimalDataRequest) => void;
}

export function AnimalAddModal({ onClose, onSubmit }: AnimalAddModalProps) {
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [model, setModel] = useState("");
  const [description, setDescription] = useState("");

  function handleSubmit(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();

    onSubmit({ name, description, model });
  }

  return (
    <Modal>
      <ModalTitle title={"Novo Animal"} description="Informe os dados do novo animal" />

      <ModalForm onSubmit={handleSubmit} >
        <ModalTextField id="animal-name" label="Nome" onChange={setName} value={name} required={true}/>          
        <ModalTextField id="animal-model" label="Modelo" onChange={setModel} value={model} required={true}/>
        <ModalLongTextField id="animal-description" label="Descrição" onChange={setDescription} value={description} required={true}/>
        <ModalFormButtons confirmLabel={"Novo"} onClose={onClose} />
      </ModalForm>
      
    </Modal>
  );
}

  onDelete: () => void;
  onOpen: () => void
}

export function AnimalCard({name, model, description, onEdit, onDelete, onOpen}: AnimalCardProps) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-8 p-8 bg-white rounded-2xl min-w-135">
      <AnimalHeader onEdit={onEdit} onDelete={onDelete}  name={name} model={model} />
      
      <Text text={description} />

      <button
        onClick={onOpen}
        className="text-main text-left text-xl hover:underline cursor-pointer">
        Gerenciar pontos de ausculta...
      </button>
    </div>
  )
}

export function AnimalDeleteModal({
  animal,
  onClose,
  onConfirm,
}: AnimalDeleteModalProps) {
  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50">

===== app/components/organisms/AnimalEditModal.tsx =====
import { useState } from "react";

import type { Animal, AnimalDataRequest } from "~/types/Animal";
import { ModalLongTextField } from "../atoms/modal/ModalLongTextField";
import { ModalTextField } from "../atoms/modal/ModalTextField";
import { ModalTitle } from "../atoms/modal/ModalTitle";
import { ModalFormButtons } from "../molecules/ModalFormButtons";
import { Modal } from "../atoms/modal/Modal";
import { ModalForm } from "../atoms/modal/ModalForm";

interface AnimalEditModalProps {
  animal?: Animal;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSubmit: (data: AnimalDataRequest) => void;
}

export function AnimalEditModal({ animal, onClose, onSubmit }: AnimalEditModalProps) {
  const [name, setName] = useState(animal?.name ?? "");
  const [model, setModel] = useState(animal?.model ?? "");
  const [description, setDescription] = useState(animal?.description ?? "");

  function handleSubmit(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();

    onSubmit({ name, description, model });
  }

  return (
    <Modal>
      <ModalTitle title={"Editar Animal"} description="Informe os dados do novo animal" />

      <ModalForm onSubmit={handleSubmit} >
        <ModalTextField id="animal-name" label="Nome" onChange={setName} value={name} required={true}/>          
        <ModalTextField id="animal-model" label="Modelo" onChange={setModel} value={model} required={true}/>
        <ModalLongTextField id="animal-description" label="Descrição" onChange={setDescription} value={description} required={true}/>
        <ModalFormButtons confirmLabel={"Editar"} onClose={onClose} />
      </ModalForm>
      
    </Modal>
  );
}

import { FaSchool } from "react-icons/fa";
import type { Institution } from "~/types/Institution";

export interface InstitutionCardProps {
  institution: Institution;
  onOpen: () => void;
}

export function InstitutionCard({ institution, onOpen }: InstitutionCardProps) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onOpen}
      className="flex w-60 cursor-pointer flex-col gap-3 rounded-2xl bg-white p-6 text-left shadow-sm transition-shadow hover:shadow-md"
    >
      <div className="flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-main">
        <FaSchool size={28} className="text-white" />
      </div>

      <span className="wrap-break-word line-clamp-2 font-bold text-slate-900 text-xl">
        {institution.name}
      </span>

      <span className="text-lg text-slate-500">
        Status:{" "}
        <span
          className={institution.active ? "text-green-700" : "text-red-700"}
        >
          {institution.active ? "Ativo" : "Desativado"}
        </span>
      </span>
    </button>
  );
}

===== app/components/organisms/PointAddModal.tsx =====
import { useState } from "react";

import type { Animal, AnimalDataRequest } from "~/types/Animal";
import { ModalLongTextField } from "../atoms/modal/ModalLongTextField";
import { ModalTextField } from "../atoms/modal/ModalTextField";
import { ModalTitle } from "../atoms/modal/ModalTitle";
import { ModalFormButtons } from "../molecules/ModalFormButtons";
import { Modal } from "../atoms/modal/Modal";
import { ModalForm } from "../atoms/modal/ModalForm";
import type { AuscultationPointRegisterRequest } from "~/types/AuscultationPoint";

interface PointAddModalProps {
  animal?: Animal;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSubmit: (data: AuscultationPointRegisterRequest) => void;
}

export function PointAddModal({ animal, onClose, onSubmit }: PointAddModalProps) {
  const [position, setPosition] = useState("");
  const animalId = `${animal?.id}`;

  function handleSubmit(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();

    onSubmit({ position, animalId });
  }

  return (
    <Modal>
      <ModalTitle
        title={"Novo Ponto de Auscultal"}
        description="Informe os dados do ponto"
      />

      <ModalForm onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
        <ModalTextField
          id="animal-name"
          label="Nome"
          onChange={setPosition}
          value={position}
          required={true}
        />
        <ModalFormButtons confirmLabel={"Novo"} onClose={onClose} />
      </ModalForm>
    </Modal>
  );
}

===== app/components/organisms/PointCard.tsx =====
import type { Scenario } from "~/types/Scenario";
import { Button } from "../atoms/Button";
import { PointHeader } from "../molecules/PointHeader";
import { ScenarioCard } from "./ScenarioCard";

export interface PointCardProps {
  position: string;
  scenarios: Scenario[];
  onPointEdit: () => void;
  onPointDelete: () => void;
  onScenarioAdd: () => void;
  onScenarioDelete: (scenario: Scenario) => void;
  onScenarioEdit: (scenario: Scenario) => void;
}

export function PointCard({
  position,
  scenarios,
  onPointEdit,
  onPointDelete,
  onScenarioAdd,
  onScenarioDelete,
  onScenarioEdit,
}: PointCardProps) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-8 p-8 bg-white rounded-2xl w-full">
      <PointHeader
        onEdit={onPointEdit}
        onDelete={onPointDelete}
        position={position}
      />

      <div className="flex w-full flex-col gap-4">
        {scenarios.map((scenario) => (
          <ScenarioCard
            key={scenario.id}
            scenario={scenario}
            onEdit={() => onScenarioEdit(scenario)}
            onDelete={() => onScenarioDelete(scenario)}
          />
        ))}
      </div>

      <Button label="Novo Cenário" onClick={onScenarioAdd} />
    </div>
  );
}

  onConfirm: () => void;
}

export function PointDeleteModal({point, onClose, onConfirm}: PointDeleteModalProps) {
  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50">
      <div className="w-125 rounded-2xl bg-white p-8">
        <ModalTitle
          title={"Remover Animal"}
          description={`Deseja remover o ponto ${point.position}?`}
        />

        <ModalText text="Essa ação não poderá ser desfeita" className="mt-8" />
        <div className="mt-12 flex gap-3 justify-end">
          <ModalButton
            label="Remover"
            onAction={onConfirm}
            className="bg-red-700"
          />
          <ModalButton
            label="Cancelar"
            onAction={onClose}
            className="bg-slate-"
          />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

} from "~/types/AuscultationPoint";

  onSubmit: (data: AuscultationPointUpdateRequest) => void;
}

export function PointEditModal({
  point,
  onClose,
  onSubmit,
}: PointEditModalProps) {
  const [position, setPosition] = useState(point.position);

        title={"Editar Ponto de Auscultal"}

  );

===== app/components/organisms/ScenarioAddModal.tsx =====
import { useState } from "react";

import type { ScenarioRegisterRequest } from "~/types/Scenario";
import { ModalForm } from "../atoms/modal/ModalForm";
import { ModalButton } from "../atoms/modal/ModalButton";
import { ModalLongTextField } from "../atoms/modal/ModalLongTextField";
import { ModalTextField } from "../atoms/modal/ModalTextField";
import { ModalFormButtons } from "../molecules/ModalFormButtons";
import { Modal } from "../atoms/modal/Modal";
import { ModalTitle } from "../atoms/modal/ModalTitle";
import { ModalFileField } from "../atoms/modal/ModalFileField";

interface ScenarioAddModalProps {
  pointId: string;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSubmit: (data: ScenarioRegisterRequest, file: File) => void;
}

export function ScenarioAddModal({
  pointId,
  onClose,
  onSubmit,
}: ScenarioAddModalProps) {
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [description, setDescription] = useState("");
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);

  function handleSubmit(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();

    if (!file) {
      return;
    }

    onSubmit({ name, description, pointId, }, file);
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50">
      <Modal>
        <ModalTitle
          title="Novo Cenário"
          description="Informe os dados do cenário"
        />
        <ModalForm onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          <ModalTextField id="scenario-name" label="Nome" value={name} onChange={setName} required/>
          <ModalLongTextField id="scenario-description" label="Descrição" value={description} onChange={setDescription} required/>
          <ModalFileField id="scenario-file" label="Arquivo de áudio" onChange={setFile} accept="audio/*" required/>
          
          <ModalFormButtons confirmLabel="Cadastrar" onClose={onClose} />
        </ModalForm>
      </Modal>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState } from "react";

import type { Scenario, ScenarioUpdateRequest } from "~/types/Scenario";

interface ScenarioEditModalProps {
  scenario: Scenario;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSubmit: (data: ScenarioUpdateRequest) => void;
}

export function ScenarioEditModal({
  scenario,
  onClose,
  onSubmit,
}: ScenarioEditModalProps) {
  const [name, setName] = useState(scenario.name);

  const [description, setDescription] = useState(scenario.description);

  function handleSubmit(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();

    onSubmit({
      name,
      description,
    });

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50">

        </h2>

            id="scenario-name"

            onChange={setName}
            required
          />

          <ModalLongTextField
            id="scenario-description"
            label="Descrição"
            value={description}
            onChange={setDescription}
            required
          />

          <ModalFormButtons confirmLabel="Editar" onClose={onClose} />
        </ModalForm>
      </div>

  );

===== app/components/organisms/TermTableRow.tsx =====
import { FiEye } from "react-icons/fi";
import type { TermResponseDTO, TermStatus } from "~/types/terms";
import { formatTermTimestamp, termStatusLabels, termTypeLabels } from "~/types/terms";

export interface TermTableRowProps {
  term: TermResponseDTO;
  onView: (term: TermResponseDTO) => void;
}

const statusBadgeStyles: Record<TermStatus, string> = {
  PUBLISHED: 'text-emerald-800',
  APPROVED: 'text-lime-800',
  IN_REVIEW: 'text-yellow-700',
  DRAFT: 'text-main/80',
  ARCHIVED: 'text-main/70',
};

export function TermTableRow({ term, onView }: TermTableRowProps) {
  return (
    <tr className='text-black border-t border-b border-gray-800/20'>
      <td className='w-1/3 px-4 py-2'>
        {termTypeLabels[term.type]}
      </td>

      <td className='w-1/6 px-4 py-2 border-l border-gray-800/20'>
        {term.version}
      </td>

      <td className='w-1/6 px-4 py-2 border-l border-gray-800/20'>
        <span className={`rounded px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium ${statusBadgeStyles[term.status]}`}>
          {termStatusLabels[term.status]}
        </span>
      </td>

      <td className='w-1/4 px-4 py-2 border-l border-gray-800/20'>
        {formatTermTimestamp(term.timestamp)}
      </td>

      <td className='w-1/12 px-4 py-2 border-l border-gray-800/20'>
        <div className='flex w-full flex-col justify-center items-center'>
          <button
            className='cursor-pointer'
            aria-label={`Visualizar ${termTypeLabels[term.type]}`}
            onClick={() => onView(term)}
          >
            <FiEye
              size={24}
              className='text-main hover:text-main/50 transition-all'
            />
          </button>
        </div>
      </td>
    </tr>
  );
}

===== app/components/organisms/Sidebar.tsx =====
import clsx from "clsx";
import { FaArrowLeft } from "react-icons/fa";
import { FiMenu } from "react-icons/fi";
import { useSidebar } from "~/hooks/useSidebar";
import Icon from "~/assets/icon.svg?react";
import { Navbar } from "../molecules/Navbar";
import { Button } from "../atoms/Button";
import { ModalButton } from "../atoms/modal/ModalButton";
import { useLogout } from "~/hooks/auth/useLogout";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Modal } from "../atoms/modal/Modal";
import { ModalForm } from "../atoms/modal/ModalForm";
import { ModalTitle } from "../atoms/modal/ModalTitle";
import { ModalFormButtons } from "../molecules/ModalFormButtons";

export function Sidebar() {
  const sidebar = useSidebar();
  const collapsed = sidebar.state === "collapse";

  const [showModal, setShowModal] = useState(false);
  const logout = useLogout();

  return (
    <aside
      className={clsx(
        "py-6 h-full bg-main transition-all overflow-hidden duration-500 flex flex-col items-center gap-8",
        collapsed ? "w-16" : "w-[22%]",
      )}
    >
      <header
        className={clsx(
          "w-full flex flex-row items-center",
          collapsed ? "justify-center" : "px-8 justify-end",
        )}
      >
        <button className="cursor-pointer" onClick={sidebar.toggleState}>
          {collapsed ? (
            <FiMenu color="#fff" size={24} />
          ) : (
            <FaArrowLeft color="#fff" size={24} />
          )}
        </button>
      </header>
      <div className="w-full h-1/4 flex justify-center items-center">
        <div
          className={clsx(
            "w-1/2 aspect-square rounded-full bg-zinc-200",
            "transition-[padding] duration-500",
            collapsed ? "p-1" : "p-10 max-xl:p-8 max-lg:p-6 max-md:p-4",
          )}
        >
          <Icon className="w-full h-full fill-main" />
        </div>
      </div>
      <Navbar collapsed={collapsed} />
      {collapsed == false && (
        <div className="w-full flex justify-start p-10">
          <ModalButton
            label="Logout"
            variant={"primary"}
            onAction={() => setShowModal(true)}
          />
        </div>
      )}
      <Modal isOpen={showModal} onClose={() => setShowModal(false)}>
        <ModalTitle title="Logout" description="Deseja sair da plataforma?" />
        <ModalForm onSubmit={logout.onLogout}>
          <ModalFormButtons
            confirmLabel="Sair"
            onClose={() => setShowModal(false)}
          />
        </ModalForm>
      </Modal>
    </aside>
  );
}

===== app/components/organisms/UserTableRow.tsx =====
import { FiTrash2 } from "react-icons/fi";
import { roleToUserRole, userRoleLabels } from "~/types/user";
import type { User } from "~/types/user";
import { useDeleteUser } from "~/hooks/users/useDeleteUser";
import { EditUserModal } from "../molecules/EditUserModal";

export interface UserTableRowProps {
  user: User;
  onChanged?: () => void;
}

export function UserTableRow({ user, onChanged }: UserTableRowProps) {
  const { deleting, deleteError, handleDelete } = useDeleteUser(user, onChanged);

  const label = userRoleLabels[roleToUserRole(user.role)];

  return (
    <tr className='text-black border-t border-b border-gray-800/20'>
      <td className="w-1/4 px-4 py-2">
        {user.name}
      </td>

      <td className="w-1/4 px-4 py-2 border-l border-gray-800/20">
        {user.email}
      </td>

      <td className="w-1/4 px-4 py-2 border-l border-gray-800/20">
        {label}
      </td>

      <td className="w-1/12 px-4 py-2 border-l border-gray-800/20">
        <div className='w-full h-full flex flex-col justify-center items-center gap-1'>
          <div className='flex flex-row justify-center items-center gap-3'>
            <EditUserModal user={user} onUpdated={onChanged} />
            <button
              className='cursor-pointer disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40'
              aria-label={`Excluir usuário ${user.name}`}
              onClick={handleDelete}
              disabled={deleting}
            >
              <FiTrash2
                size={24}
                className='text-main hover:text-main/50 transition-all'
              />
            </button>
          </div>
          {deleteError && (
            <span className='text-xs text-red-500 text-center'>
              {deleteError}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
      </td>
    </tr>
  )
}

===== app/components/organisms/dashboard/PendingDevicesCard.tsx =====
import { useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "~/components/atoms/Button";
import { TableHeadColumn } from "~/components/atoms/TableHeadColumn";
import { Card } from "~/components/atoms/card/Card";
import { Alert } from "~/components/atoms/page/Alert";
import { SelectInput } from "~/components/atoms/input/SelectInput";
import type { DeviceInfo } from "~/types/device";
import type { Institution } from "~/types/Institution";
import { formatDateTime } from "~/utils/dashboardFormat";

export interface PendingDevicesCardProps {
  devices: DeviceInfo[];
  institutions: Institution[];
  loading: boolean;
  error: string | null;
  approvingId: string | null;
  onApprove: (deviceId: string, institutionId: string) => void;
}

/** Fila de aprovação: o admin escolhe a instituição e aprova o ESP. */
export function PendingDevicesCard({
  devices,
  institutions,
  loading,
  error,
  approvingId,
  onApprove,
}: PendingDevicesCardProps) {
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});

  const options = new Map<string, string>(
    institutions
      .filter((institution) => institution.active)
      .map((institution) => [institution.id, institution.name]),
  );

  return (
    <Card>
      <header className="flex flex-col gap-1">
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">
          Aguardando aprovação
        </h2>
        <p className="text-slate-500">
          ESPs que se registraram sozinhos. Escolha a instituição para liberar.
        </p>
      </header>

      {error && <Alert message={error} type="error" />}

      {loading && devices.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="text-slate-500">Carregando...</p>
      ) : devices.length === 0 ? (
        <Alert message="Nenhum dispositivo aguardando aprovação." type="muted" />
      ) : (
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full min-w-3xl text-left">
            <thead className="bg-main text-white">
              <tr>
                <TableHeadColumn className="rounded-tl-md">
                  Identificador
                </TableHeadColumn>
                <TableHeadColumn>Nome enviado</TableHeadColumn>
                <TableHeadColumn>Registrado</TableHeadColumn>
                <TableHeadColumn>Instituição</TableHeadColumn>
                <TableHeadColumn className="rounded-tr-md" />
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {devices.map((device) => {
                const institutionId = selected[device.id] ?? "";
                const approving = approvingId === device.id;

                return (
                  <tr
                    key={device.id}
                    className="border-b border-gray-800/20 text-black"
                  >
                    <td className="px-6 py-3 font-mono text-sm">
                      {device.hardwareId}
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-6 py-3">{device.name}</td>
                    <td className="px-6 py-3 whitespace-nowrap">
                      {formatDateTime(device.createdAt)}
                    </td>
                    <td className="min-w-56 px-6 py-3">
                      <SelectInput
                        id={`institution-${device.id}`}
                        name={`institution-${device.id}`}
                        placeholder="Escolha a instituição"
                        options={options}
                        value={institutionId}
                        onChange={(event) =>
                          setSelected((current) => ({
                            ...current,
                            [device.id]: event.target.value,
                          }))
                        }
                      />
                    </td>
                    <td className="w-32 px-6 py-3">
                      <Button
                        label={approving ? "Aprovando..." : "Aprovar"}
                        disabled={!institutionId || approving}
                        onClick={() => onApprove(device.id, institutionId)}
                      />
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                );
              })}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}
    </Card>
  );
}

import { ModalButton } from "../atoms/modal/ModalButton";

export interface ModalFormButtonsProps {
  confirmLabel: string;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export function ModalFormButtons({
  confirmLabel,
  onClose,
}: ModalFormButtonsProps) {
  return (
    <div className="mt-3 flex gap-3 justify-end">
      <button
        type="button"
        className="cursor-pointer bg-stone-300 rounded-lg px-6 py-3"
        onClick={onClose}
      >
        <p className="text-slate-900 text-xl line-clamp-3 wrap-break-word">
          Cancelar
        </p>
      </button>

      <button
        type="submit"
        className="cursor-pointer bg-[#3730A3] rounded-lg px-6 py-3 ${className}"
      >
        <p className=" text-white text-xl line-clamp-3 wrap-break-word">
          {confirmLabel}
        </p>
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

===== app/components/molecules/EditUserModal.tsx =====
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { FiEdit2 } from "react-icons/fi";
import type { User } from "~/types/user";
import { useUpdateUser } from "~/hooks/users/useUpdateUser";
import { TextInput } from "../atoms/input/TextInput";
import { Modal } from "../atoms/modal/Modal";
import { ModalTitle } from "../atoms/modal/ModalTitle";
import { ModalForm } from "../atoms/modal/ModalForm";
import { ModalButton } from "../atoms/modal/ModalButton";

export interface EditUserModalProps {
  user: User;
  onUpdated?: () => void;
}

export function EditUserModal({ user, onUpdated }: EditUserModalProps) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const form = useUpdateUser(user, onUpdated);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (form.succeeded) {
      setOpen(false);
    }
  }, [form.succeeded]);

  function handleOpen() {
    setOpen(true);
    form.reset();
  }

  return (
    <>
      <button
        className='cursor-pointer'
        aria-label={`Editar usuário ${user.name}`}
        onClick={handleOpen}
      >
        <FiEdit2
          size={24}
          className='text-main hover:text-main/50 transition-all'
        />
      </button>

      {open && (
        <Modal>
          <ModalTitle
            title='Editar usuário'
            description='Altere os dados do usuário.'
          />

          <ModalForm onSubmit={form.handleSubmit}>
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
              <p>Nome</p>
              <TextInput
                placeholder='Digite o nome'
                value={form.name}
                onChange={(event) => form.setName(event)} id={""} name={""} type={""}              />
              {form.fieldErrors.name && (
                <span className="text-sm text-red-500">
                  {form.fieldErrors.name}
                </span>
              )}
            </div>

            {form.generalError && (
              <p className="text-sm text-red-500">{form.generalError}</p>
            )}

            <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
              <ModalButton
                label='Cancelar'
                onAction={() => setOpen(false)}
                className='bg-stone-300'
              />

              <button
                type='submit'
                className="rounded bg-main px-4 py-2 text-white cursor-pointer disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
                disabled={form.submitting}
              >
                {form.submitting ? "Salvando..." : "Salvar"}
              </button>
            </div>
          </ModalForm>
        </Modal>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

===== app/components/molecules/Navbar.tsx =====
import clsx from "clsx";
import { FaDog, FaSchool } from "react-icons/fa";
import { FiBarChart2, FiFileText, FiSearch, FiSliders, FiUser } from "react-icons/fi";
import { NavbarButton } from "../atoms/NavbarButton";
import { useAuth } from "~/context/AuthContext";

export interface NavbarProps {
  collapsed: boolean;
}

export function Navbar({ collapsed }: NavbarProps) {
  const { user } = useAuth();

  return (
      <ul className={clsx(
        'w-full flex flex-col gap-2',
        'transition-[padding] duration-500',
        collapsed ? 'pl-0' : 'pl-10'
      )}>
        {(user?.role == "ADMIN" || user?.role == "MANAGER") && (
          <NavbarButton
            to='/dashboard/overview'
            label='Painel'
            collapse={collapsed}
            icon={FiBarChart2}
          />
        )}
        <NavbarButton
          to='/dashboard/devices'
          label='Dispositivos'
          collapse={collapsed}
          icon={FaDog}
          end
        />
        <NavbarButton
          to='/dashboard/terms'
          label='Termos'
          collapse={collapsed}
          icon={FiFileText}
        />
        <NavbarButton
          to='/dashboard/audit'
          label='Auditoria'
          collapse={collapsed}
          icon={FiSearch}
        />
        <NavbarButton
          to='/dashboard/schools'
          label='Instituições'
          collapse={collapsed}
          icon={FaSchool}
        />
        {user?.role == "ADMIN" && (
          <NavbarButton
            to='/dashboard/admins'
            label='Administradores'
            collapse={collapsed}
            icon={FiUser}
          />
        )}
        {user?.role == "MANAGER" && (
          <NavbarButton
            to={`/dashboard/users/${user.institutionId}`}
            label='Membros'
            collapse={collapsed}
            icon={FiUser}
          />
        )}
        <NavbarButton
          to='/dashboard/scene'
          label='Cenários'
          collapse={collapsed}
          icon={FiSliders}
        />
      </ul>
  )
}

===== app/components/molecules/controlled/ControlledCodeField.tsx =====
import {
  Controller,
  type Control,
  type FieldValues,
  type Path,
  type RegisterOptions,
} from "react-hook-form";
import { TextField } from "../input/TextField";
import { CodeField } from "../input/CodeField";

export interface ControlledCodeFieldProps<T extends FieldValues> {
  id: string;
  name: Path<T>;
  label: string;
  control: Control<T>;
  length: number,
  rules?: RegisterOptions<T, Path<T>>;
  onSubmit: () => void
}

export function ControlledCodeField<T extends FieldValues>({
  id,
  name,
  label,
  length,
  control,
  rules,
  onSubmit
}: ControlledCodeFieldProps<T>) {
  return (
    <Controller
      name={name}
      control={control}
      rules={{
        ...rules,
      }}
      render={({ field, fieldState: { error } }) => (
        <CodeField
          id={id}
          name={field.name}
          label={label}
          value={field.value}
          onChange={field.onChange}
          length={length}
          error={error?.message}
          onSubmit={() => onSubmit()}
        />
      )}
    />
  );
}

===== app/components/molecules/controlled/ControlledEmailField.tsx =====
import {
  Controller,
  type Control,
  type FieldValues,
  type Path,
  type RegisterOptions,
} from "react-hook-form";
import { TextField } from "../input/TextField";

export interface ControlledEmailFieldProps<T extends FieldValues> {
  id: string;
  name: Path<T>;
  label: string;
  placeholder: string;
  control: Control<T>;
  rules?: RegisterOptions<T, Path<T>>;
}

export function ControlledEmailField<T extends FieldValues>({
  id,
  name,
  label,
  placeholder,
  control,
  rules,
}: ControlledEmailFieldProps<T>) {
  return (
    <Controller
      name={name}
      control={control}
      rules={{
        pattern: {
          value: /^(?!.*[A-Z])[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/,
          message: "Informe um e-mail válido",
        },
        ...rules
      }}
      render={({ field, fieldState: { error } }) => (
        <TextField
          id={id}
          name={field.name}
          label={label}
          placeholder={placeholder}
          type="email"
          value={field.value}
          error={error?.message}
          onChange={field.onChange}
          onBlur={field.onBlur}
          ref={field.ref}
        />
      )}
    />
  );
}

===== app/components/molecules/users/AddAdminModal.tsx =====
import { useState } from "react";
import { FiPlus } from "react-icons/fi";
import { userRoleLabels, userRoles } from "~/types/user";
import { useCreateUser } from "~/hooks/users/useCreateUser";
import { TextInput } from "../../atoms/input/TextInput";
import { Modal } from "../../atoms/modal/Modal";
import { ModalTitle } from "../../atoms/modal/ModalTitle";
import { ModalForm } from "../../atoms/modal/ModalForm";
import { ModalButton } from "../../atoms/modal/ModalButton";
import { TextField } from "../input/TextField";
import { Controller, useForm } from "react-hook-form";
import { ControlledTextField } from "../controlled/ControlledTextField";
import { ControlledEmailField } from "../controlled/ControlledEmailField";
import { ControlledSelectField } from "../controlled/ControlledSelectField";

export interface AddAdminModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}

interface FormData {
  name: string;
  email: string;
}

export function AddAdminModal({ isOpen, onClose }: AddAdminModalProps) {
  const { control, handleSubmit } = useForm<FormData>();

  const { error, loading, success, setSuccess, handleCreateAdminUserSubmit } =
    useCreateUser();

  return (
    <Modal isOpen={isOpen} onClose={onClose}>
      {success ? (
        <>
          <ModalTitle
            title="Convite Enviado"
            description="Um convite foi enviado ao e-mail do usuário para finalização do cadastro"
          />
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
            <div className="flex justify-end">
              <ModalButton
                label="Fechar"
                variant="primary"
                onAction={() => {
                  setSuccess(false);
                  onClose();
                }}
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        </>
      ) : (
        <>
          <ModalTitle
            title="Cadastrar Usuário"
            description="Digite os dados do novo usuário"
          />
          <ModalForm onSubmit={handleSubmit(handleCreateAdminUserSubmit)}>
            <ControlledTextField
              id="name"
              name="name"
              label="Nome"
              placeholder="Digite o nome"
              control={control}
              rules={{
                required: "Nome obrigatório",
              }}
            />

            <ControlledEmailField
              id="email"
              name="email"
              label="E-mail"
              placeholder="Digite o e-mail"
              control={control}
              rules={{
                required: "E-mail obrigatório",
              }}
            />

            {error && <span className="text-red-500 text-sm">{error}</span>}

            <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
              <ModalButton
                label="Fechar"
                variant="secondary"
                onAction={onClose}
              />

              <ModalButton
                type="submit"
                label={loading ? "Enviando" : "Salvar"}
                disabled={loading}
                variant="primary"
              />
            </div>
          </ModalForm>
        </>
      )}
    </Modal>
  );
}

===== app/components/molecules/users/AddInstitutionalUserModal.tsx =====
import { use, useState } from "react";
import { FiPlus } from "react-icons/fi";
import {
  userRoleLabels,
  userRoles,
  type CreateInstitutionalUserRequest,
} from "~/types/user";
import { useCreateUser } from "~/hooks/users/useCreateUser";
import { TextInput } from "../../atoms/input/TextInput";
import { Modal } from "../../atoms/modal/Modal";
import { ModalTitle } from "../../atoms/modal/ModalTitle";
import { ModalForm } from "../../atoms/modal/ModalForm";
import { ModalButton } from "../../atoms/modal/ModalButton";
import { TextField } from "../input/TextField";
import { Controller, useForm } from "react-hook-form";
import { ControlledTextField } from "../controlled/ControlledTextField";
import { ControlledEmailField } from "../controlled/ControlledEmailField";
import { ControlledSelectField } from "../controlled/ControlledSelectField";
import { useAuth } from "~/context/AuthContext";

export interface AddInstitutionalUserModalProps {
  institutionId: string;
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export function AddInstitutionalUserModal({
  institutionId,
  isOpen,
  onClose,
}: AddInstitutionalUserModalProps) {
  const { control, register, handleSubmit } =
    useForm<CreateInstitutionalUserRequest>({
      defaultValues: {
        email: "",
        name: "",
        institutionId: institutionId,
        role: "student",
      },
    });

  const options = new Map<string, string>();
  options.set("student", "Aluno")
  options.set("manager", "Professor")
  options.set("teacher", "Gestor")

  const { error, loading, success, setSuccess, handleCreateInstitutionalUserSubmit } =
    useCreateUser();

  return (
    <Modal isOpen={isOpen} onClose={onClose}>
      {success ? (
        <>
          <ModalTitle
            title="Convite Enviado"
            description="Um convite foi enviado ao e-mail do usuário para finalização do cadastro"
          />
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
            <div className="flex justify-end">
              <ModalButton
                label="Fechar"
                variant="primary"
                onAction={() => {
                  setSuccess(false);
                  onClose();
                }}
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        </>
      ) : (
        <>
          <ModalTitle
            title="Cadastrar Usuário"
            description="Digite os dados do novo usuário"
          />
          <ModalForm onSubmit={handleSubmit(handleCreateInstitutionalUserSubmit)}>
            <input
              type="hidden"
              {...register("institutionId", { value: institutionId })}
            />

            <ControlledTextField
              id="name"
              name="name"
              label="Nome"
              placeholder="Digite o nome"
              control={control}
              rules={{
                required: "Nome obrigatório",
              }}
            />

            <ControlledEmailField
              id="email"
              name="email"
              label="E-mail"
              placeholder="Digite o e-mail"
              control={control}
              rules={{
                required: "E-mail obrigatório",
              }}
            />

            <ControlledSelectField
              id="role"
              name="role"
              label="Função"
              options={options}
              placeholder={"Selecione uma função"}
              rules={{
                required: "Função obrigatória",
              }}
              control={control}
            />

            {error && <span className="text-red-500 text-sm">{error}</span>}

            <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
              <ModalButton
                label="Fechar"
                variant="secondary"
                onAction={onClose}
              />

              <ModalButton
                type="submit"
                label={loading ? "Enviando" : "Salvar"}
                disabled={loading}
                variant="primary"
              />
            </div>
          </ModalForm>
        </>
      )}
    </Modal>
  );
}

===== app/components/molecules/users/UserCard.tsx =====
import { FaUser, FaUserGroup } from "react-icons/fa6";
import { MdOutlineEmail } from "react-icons/md";

export interface UserCardProps {
  name: string;
  email: string;
  role: string;
}

export function UserCard({ name, email, role }: UserCardProps) {
  let labelRole = "";
  if (role === "STUDENT") 
    labelRole = "Estudante";
  else if (role === "MANAGER")
    labelRole = "Gestor";
  else if (role === "TEACHER")
    labelRole = "Professor";
  
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-row gap-4 items-center rounded-lg p-5 w-full bg-white">
      
      <div className="flex size-16 aspect-square shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-main text-white">
        <span className="text-2xl font-bold">{name.charAt(0)}</span>
      </div>

      <div className="min-w-0 w-full flex flex-col justify-between">
        <p className="font-bold text-xl text-black max-w-full truncate">
          {name}
        </p>

        <p className="flex flex-row gap-2 items-center text-black">
          <span className="text-xl font-bold align-middle truncate">{email}</span>
        </p>

        <p className="px-4 py-0.5 flex gap-2 items-center w-fit text-white bg-main rounded-full">
          <FaUser size={16} />
          <span className="text-base truncate">{labelRole}</span>
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

        )}
        <NavbarButton
          to='/dashboard/scene'
          label='Cenários'
          collapse={collapsed}
          icon={FiSliders}
        />
      </ul>
  )
}

===== app/components/molecules/dashboard/DeviceStatusChart.tsx =====
import type { InstitutionDevices } from "~/types/dashboard";
import { formatNumber } from "~/utils/dashboardFormat";

export interface DeviceStatusChartProps {
  institutions: InstitutionDevices[];
}

/**
 * Uma barra por instituição: operando, ACTIVE bloqueado pela instituição e
 * INACTIVE. (PENDING não tem instituição, aparece só no resumo.)
 */
export function DeviceStatusChart({ institutions }: DeviceStatusChartProps) {
  if (institutions.length === 0) {
    return (
      <p className="text-slate-500">Nenhum dispositivo associado a instituições.</p>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <ul className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        {institutions.map((row) => {
          const blocked = row.active - row.operating;
          const total = Math.max(1, row.active + row.inactive);

          return (
            <li key={row.institutionId} className="flex flex-col gap-1">
              <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-4">
                <span className="line-clamp-1 font-semibold text-slate-900">
                  {row.institutionName}
                </span>
                <span className="shrink-0 text-sm tabular-nums text-slate-500">
                  {formatNumber(row.active + row.inactive)}
                </span>
              </div>

              <div
                className="flex h-3 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-slate-100"
                role="img"
                aria-label={`${row.operating} operando, ${blocked} bloqueados pela instituição, ${row.inactive} inativos`}
              >
                <span
                  className="bg-main"
                  style={{ width: `${(row.operating / total) * 100}%` }}
                />
                <span
                  className="bg-amber-500"
                  style={{ width: `${(blocked / total) * 100}%` }}
                />
                <span
                  className="bg-red-400"
                  style={{ width: `${(row.inactive / total) * 100}%` }}
                />
              </div>

              {!row.institutionActive && (
                <span className="text-sm text-amber-700">
                  Instituição inativa ou removida: dispositivos bloqueados.
                </span>
              )}
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ul>

      <ul className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
        <li><i className="mr-1 inline-block size-2.5 rounded-sm bg-main" />Operando</li>
        <li><i className="mr-1 inline-block size-2.5 rounded-sm bg-amber-500" />Bloqueado pela instituição</li>
        <li><i className="mr-1 inline-block size-2.5 rounded-sm bg-red-400" />Inativo</li>
      </ul>
    </div>
  );
}

===== app/components/molecules/dashboard/RankingList.tsx =====
import { formatNumber } from "~/utils/dashboardFormat";

export interface RankingItem {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  value: number;
  detail?: string;
}

export interface RankingListProps {
  items: RankingItem[];
  emptyMessage: string;
}

export function RankingList({ items, emptyMessage }: RankingListProps) {
  if (items.length === 0) {
    return <p className="text-slate-500">{emptyMessage}</p>;
  }

  const max = Math.max(1, ...items.map((item) => item.value));

  return (
    <ol className="flex flex-col gap-3">
      {items.map((item) => (
        <li key={item.id} className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-4">
            <span className="line-clamp-1 text-slate-900">{item.label}</span>
            <span className="shrink-0 tabular-nums text-slate-500">
              {formatNumber(item.value)}
              {item.detail ? ` · ${item.detail}` : ""}
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-slate-100">
            <span
              className="block h-full bg-main"
              style={{ width: `${(item.value / max) * 100}%` }}
            />
          </div>
        </li>
      ))}
    </ol>
  );
}

===== app/components/molecules/dashboard/UsageChart.tsx =====
import type { DailyUsage } from "~/types/dashboard";
import { formatDay, formatNumber } from "~/utils/dashboardFormat";

export interface UsageChartProps {
  days: DailyUsage[];
}

const WIDTH = 600;
const HEIGHT = 160;

/** Barras de sessões (eventos PLAY) por dia, em SVG, sem biblioteca de gráfico. */
export function UsageChart({ days }: UsageChartProps) {
  if (days.length === 0) return null;

  const max = Math.max(1, ...days.map((day) => day.plays));
  const slot = WIDTH / days.length;
  const bar = Math.max(1, slot * 0.7);
  const middle = days[Math.floor(days.length / 2)];

  return (
    <svg
      viewBox={`0 0 ${WIDTH} ${HEIGHT + 22}`}
      className="h-auto w-full overflow-visible"
      role="img"
      aria-label="Sessões de ausculta por dia"
    >
      {[0, 0.5, 1].map((step) => (
        <line
          key={step}
          x1={0}
          x2={WIDTH}
          y1={HEIGHT - HEIGHT * step}
          y2={HEIGHT - HEIGHT * step}
          className="stroke-slate-200"
        />
      ))}

      {days.map((day, index) => {
        const height = (day.plays / max) * HEIGHT;
        return (
          <rect
            key={day.date}
            x={index * slot + (slot - bar) / 2}
            y={HEIGHT - height}
            width={bar}
            height={height}
            rx={2}
            className="fill-main"
          >
            <title>{`${formatDay(day.date)}: ${formatNumber(day.plays)} sessões`}</title>
          </rect>
        );
      })}

      <text x={0} y={10} className="fill-slate-500 text-[10px]">
        {formatNumber(max)}
      </text>
      <text x={0} y={HEIGHT + 16} className="fill-slate-500 text-[10px]">
        {formatDay(days[0].date)}
      </text>
      <text
        x={WIDTH / 2}
        y={HEIGHT + 16}
        textAnchor="middle"
        className="fill-slate-500 text-[10px]"
      >
        {formatDay(middle.date)}
      </text>
      <text
        x={WIDTH}
        y={HEIGHT + 16}
        textAnchor="end"
        className="fill-slate-500 text-[10px]"
      >
        {formatDay(days[days.length - 1].date)}
      </text>
    </svg>
  );
}

===== app/components/atoms/NavbarButton.tsx =====
import clsx from "clsx";
import type { IconType } from "react-icons";
import { NavLink } from "react-router";

export interface NavbarButtonProps {
  to: string;
  label: string;
  collapse: boolean;
  icon: IconType;
  end?: boolean;
}

export function NavbarButton(props: NavbarButtonProps) {
  const Icon = props.icon;
  return (
    <NavLink
      end={props.end}
      to={props.to}
      className={({ isActive }) => clsx(
        'relative gap-3 flex flex-row transition-[border-radius]',
        !props.collapse ?
          'rounded-l-xl px-6 py-4' :
          'p-4 justify-center items-center',
        isActive ?
          'bg-zinc-200 fill-main text-main' :
          'bg-main fill-white text-white',
        isActive ? `
        before:absolute
        before:-right-2
        before:-top-2
        before:size-4
        before:bg-main
        before:border-zinc-200
        before:border-r-8
        before:border-b-8
        before:rounded-br-4xl

        after:absolute
        after:-right-2
        after:-bottom-2
        after:size-4
        after:bg-main
        after:border-zinc-200
        after:border-r-8
        after:border-t-8
        after:rounded-tr-4xl
        ` : '',
      )}
      >
        <Icon
          size={24}
        />
        {!props.collapse &&
          <span className='font-semibold w-full overflow-hidden'>{props.label}</span>}
    </NavLink>
  )
}

===== app/components/atoms/modal/Modal.tsx =====
import type React from "react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { IoClose } from "react-icons/io5";

export interface ModalProps{
  children: React.ReactNode
  isOpen: boolean
  onClose: () => void
  className?: string
}

export function Modal({children, className, isOpen, onClose}: ModalProps) {
  if (!isOpen) return;
  
  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50">
      <div className={clsx('relative rounded-2xl bg-white p-8 pt-12', className ?? 'w-150')}>
        <button 
          type="button" 
          onClick={onClose}
          className="absolute top-6 right-6 text-gray-500 hover:text-black transition-colors"
        >
          <IoClose size={24} />
        </button>

        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
===== app/components/atoms/modal/ModalButton.tsx =====
import type { ComponentProps } from "react";

export interface ModalButtonProps extends ComponentProps<"button"> {
  label: string;
  variant: "primary" | "secondary" | "danger";
  onAction?: () => void;
}

export function ModalButton({
  label,
  type = "button",
  variant = "secondary",
  className = "",
  disabled,
  onAction,
  ...props
}: ModalButtonProps) {

  const variants = {
    primary: "bg-main text-white",
    secondary: "bg-gray-300 text-black",
    danger: "bg-red-600 text-white",
  };

  return (
    <button
      {...props}
      type={type}
      className={`cursor-pointer rounded px-4 py-2
        ${variants[variant]}
        ${disabled ? "cursor-not-allowed opacity-50" : ""}
        ${className}`}
      onClick={onAction}
      disabled={disabled}
    >
      {label}
    </button>
  );
}

===== app/components/atoms/Markdown.tsx =====
import ReactMarkdown from "react-markdown";
import remarkGfm from "remark-gfm";

export interface MarkdownProps {
  content: string;
}

export function Markdown({ content }: MarkdownProps) {
  return (
    <div className='text-slate-700 text-base leading-relaxed'>
      <ReactMarkdown
        remarkPlugins={[remarkGfm]}
        components={{
          h1: ({ node, ...props }) => (
            <h1 className='text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 mt-4 mb-3' {...props} />
          ),
          h2: ({ node, ...props }) => (
            <h2 className='text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 mt-4 mb-2' {...props} />
          ),
          h3: ({ node, ...props }) => (
            <h3 className='text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900 mt-3 mb-2' {...props} />
          ),
          p: ({ node, ...props }) => <p className='my-2' {...props} />,
          a: ({ node, ...props }) => (
            <a className='text-main underline' target='_blank' rel='noreferrer' {...props} />
          ),
          strong: ({ node, ...props }) => (
            <strong className='font-semibold text-slate-900' {...props} />
          ),
          ul: ({ node, ...props }) => (
            <ul className='my-2 list-disc space-y-1 pl-6' {...props} />
          ),
          ol: ({ node, ...props }) => (
            <ol className='my-2 list-decimal space-y-1 pl-6' {...props} />
          ),
          blockquote: ({ node, ...props }) => (
            <blockquote
              className='my-3 border-l-4 border-main/40 pl-4 italic text-slate-600'
              {...props}
            />
          ),
          code: ({ node, ...props }) => (
            <code className='rounded bg-zinc-100 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-sm text-slate-800' {...props} />
          ),
          hr: () => <hr className='my-4 border-zinc-200' />,
          table: ({ node, ...props }) => (
            <div className='my-3 overflow-x-auto'>
              <table className='w-full border-collapse text-sm' {...props} />
            </div>
          ),
          th: ({ node, ...props }) => (
            <th className='border border-zinc-300 bg-zinc-100 px-3 py-2 text-left font-semibold text-slate-900' {...props} />
          ),
          td: ({ node, ...props }) => (
            <td className='border border-zinc-300 px-3 py-2' {...props} />
          ),
        }}
      >
        {content}
      </ReactMarkdown>
    </div>
  );
}

===== app/components/atoms/card/CardHeader.tsx =====
import { FiEdit, FiTrash2 } from "react-icons/fi";
import { ButtonIcon } from "../ButtonIcon";

export interface CardHeaderProps {
  icon?: string;
  title: string;
  description?: string;
  onEdit?: () => void;
  onDelete?: () => void;
}

export function CardHeader({ icon, title, description, onEdit, onDelete }: CardHeaderProps) {
  return (
    <div className="flex gap-8">
      {icon != null && (
        <div className="flex justify-center w-20 h-20 bg-main rounded-2xl min-w-24 min-h-24">
          <img src={icon} alt="" className="w-18" />
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="flex flex-col gap-1 w-2xs max-w-56">
        <h3 className="wrap-break-word font-bold text-slate-900 text-2xl line-clamp-2">
          {title}
        </h3>
        {description != null && (
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-xl line-clamp-1">{description}</p>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="flex gap-8">
        {onEdit != null && (
          <ButtonIcon onClick={onEdit}>
            <FiEdit size={32} className="text-slate-900" />
          </ButtonIcon>
        )}

        {onDelete != null && (
          <ButtonIcon onClick={onDelete}>
            <FiTrash2 size={32} className="text-red-900" />
          </ButtonIcon>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== app/components/atoms/auth/AdminLayout.tsx =====
import type { PropsWithChildren } from "react";
import { useLocation, Navigate, Outlet } from "react-router";
import { useAuth } from "~/context/AuthContext";
import { LoadingPage } from "../../../pages/LoadingPage";

export default function AdminLayout() {
  const { user, loading } = useAuth();

  if (loading) {
    return <LoadingPage />;
  }

  if (!user) {
    return <Navigate to="/" replace />;
  }

  if (user.role != "ADMIN") {
    return <Navigate to="/forbidden" replace />;
  }

  return <Outlet />;
}

===== app/components/atoms/auth/ProtectedLayout.tsx =====
import type { PropsWithChildren } from "react";
import { useLocation, Navigate, useNavigate } from "react-router";
import { useAuth } from "~/context/AuthContext";
import { LoadingPage } from "../../../pages/LoadingPage";
import type React from "react";

export interface ProtectedLayoutProps {
  children: React.ReactNode;
}

export default function ProtectedLayout({ children }: ProtectedLayoutProps) {
    const { user, loading } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate()

  if (loading) {
    return <LoadingPage />;
  }

  if (!user) {
    return <Navigate to="/" replace />;
  }

  return <>{children}</>
}

===== app/components/atoms/auth/StaffLayout.tsx =====
import { Navigate, Outlet } from "react-router";
import { useAuth } from "~/context/AuthContext";
import { LoadingPage } from "../../../pages/LoadingPage";

/** Rotas de ADMIN e MANAGER (a dashboard não existe para professor e estudante). */
export default function StaffLayout() {
  const { user, loading } = useAuth();

  if (loading) {
    return <LoadingPage />;
  }

  if (!user) {
    return <Navigate to="/" replace />;
  }

  if (user.role !== "ADMIN" && user.role !== "MANAGER") {
    return <Navigate to="/forbidden" replace />;
  }

  return <Outlet />;
}

===== app/components/atoms/input/CodeInput.tsx =====
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";

export interface CodeInputProps {
  length: number;
  value: string;
  error?: string;
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
  onSubmit: (code: string) => void;
}

export function CodeInput({
  length,
  value,
  error,
  onChange,
  onSubmit,
}: CodeInputProps) {
  const [code, setCode] = useState<string[]>(
    value ? value.split("") : new Array(length).fill(""),
  );

  const inputRefs = useRef<(HTMLInputElement | null)[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (value !== undefined) {
      setCode(
        value
          .split("")
          .concat(new Array(Math.max(0, length - value.length)).fill("")),
      );
    }
  }, [value, length]);

  const handleChange = (inputValue: string, index: number) => {
    const digit = inputValue.replace(/[^0-9]/g, "").slice(-1);

    const newCode = [...code];
    newCode[index] = digit;
    setCode(newCode);

    if (digit && index < length - 1 && inputRefs.current[index + 1]) {
      inputRefs.current[index + 1]?.focus();
    }

    const fullCode = newCode.join("");

    onChange?.(fullCode);

    if (fullCode.length === length) {
      onSubmit(fullCode);
    }
  };

  const handleKeyDown = (
    e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>,
    index: number,
  ) => {
    if (e.key === "Backspace") {
      const newCode = [...code];

      if (!code[index] && index > 0 && inputRefs.current[index - 1]) {
        inputRefs.current[index - 1]?.focus();
      } else if (code[index]) {
        newCode[index] = "";
        setCode(newCode);
        onChange?.(newCode.join(""));
      }
    }
  };

  const setInputRef = (el: HTMLInputElement | null, index: number) => {
    inputRefs.current[index] = el;
  };

  const handleClick = (index: number) => {
    inputRefs.current[index]?.setSelectionRange(1, 1);

    if (index > 0 && !code[index - 1]) {
      inputRefs.current[code.indexOf("")]?.focus();
    }
  };

  const handlePaste = (e: React.ClipboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const pastedData = e.clipboardData.getData("text").replace(/[^0-9]/g, "");

    if (!pastedData) return;

    const digits = pastedData.slice(0, length).split("");
    const newCode = [...code];

    digits.forEach((digit, i) => {
      newCode[i] = digit;
    });

    setCode(newCode);
    onChange?.(newCode.join(""));

    const nextIndex = Math.min(digits.length, length - 1);
    inputRefs.current[nextIndex]?.focus();

    const fullCode = newCode.join("");
    if (fullCode.length === length) {
      onSubmit(fullCode);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (inputRefs.current[0]) {
      inputRefs.current[0].focus();
    }
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
      <div className="flex gap-2">
        {code.map((val, index) => {
          return (
            <input
              key={index}
              type="text"
              inputMode="numeric"
              maxLength={1}
              value={val}
              ref={(el) => setInputRef(el, index)}
              onChange={(e) => handleChange(e.target.value, index)}
              onKeyDown={(e) => handleKeyDown(e, index)}
              onClick={() => handleClick(index)}
              onPaste={handlePaste}
              className={`size-12 text-center text-lg border text-slate-700 border-black rounded 
                        ${error ? "border-red-500 focus:border-red-600" : "border-slate-300 focus:border-slate-500"}`}
            />
          );
        })}
      </div>
      {error && <span className="text-red-500 text-sm">{error}</span>}
    </div>
  );
}

===== app/components/atoms/input/PasswordInput.tsx =====
import { useState, type Ref } from "react";
import { FiEye, FiEyeOff } from "react-icons/fi";

export interface PasswordInputProps {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  placeholder: string;
  value: string;
  error?: string;
  ref?: Ref<HTMLInputElement>;
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
  onBlur?: () => void;
}

export function PasswordInput({
  id,
  name,
  placeholder,
  error,
  ref,
  onChange,
  onBlur,
}: PasswordInputProps) {
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);


  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
      <div className="relative flex items-center w-full">
        <input
          id={id}
          name={name}
          type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"}
          onChange={(event) => onChange(event.target.value)}
          onBlur={onBlur}
          placeholder={placeholder}
          ref={ref}
          className={`
                    w-full px-4 py-3 pr-12 rounded-lg border
                    text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400
                    focus:outline-none transition-colors
                    ${error ? "border-red-500 focus:border-red-600" : "border-slate-300 focus:border-slate-500"}
                  `}
        />

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setShowPassword((value) => !value)}
          tabIndex={-1}
          className="
                    absolute right-4
                    text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-700
                    focus:outline-none
                    flex items-center justify-center
                  "
        >
          {showPassword ? <FiEye size={20} /> : <FiEyeOff size={20} />}
        </button>
      </div>

      {error && <span className="text-red-500 text-sm">{error}</span>}
    </div>
  );
}

===== app/components/atoms/input/SelectInput.tsx =====
import type { ChangeEvent, Ref } from "react";

export interface SelectInputProps {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  placeholder: string;
  options: Map<string, string | number>;
  error?: string;
  value: string | number;
  ref?: Ref<HTMLSelectElement>;
  onChange?: (e: ChangeEvent<HTMLSelectElement>) => void;
  onBlur?: () => void;
}

export function SelectInput({
  id,
  name,
  placeholder,
  options,
  error,
  ref,
  value,
  onChange,
  onBlur,
}: SelectInputProps) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
      <select
        id={id}
        name={name}
        ref={ref}
        onBlur={onBlur}
        value={value}
        onChange={onChange}
        className={`
          w-full px-4 py-3 rounded-lg border
          text-slate-900 bg-transparent appearance-none
          focus:outline-none transition-colors cursor-pointer
          ${
            error
              ? "border-red-500 focus:border-red-600"
              : "border-slate-300 focus:border-slate-500"
          }
        `}
      >
        <option disabled value="">
          {placeholder}
        </option>

        {Array.from(options.entries()).map(([optionValue, label]) => (
          <option key={label} value={optionValue}>
            {label}
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>

      {error && <span className="text-red-500 text-sm">{error}</span>}
    </div>
  );
}

export interface AlertProps {
  message: string;
  type: "success" | "error" | "muted";
}

export function Alert({ message, type }: AlertProps) {
  const styles = {
    success: "border-green-500 bg-emerald-50 text-green-500",
    error: "border-red-500 bg-red-50 text-red-500",
    muted: "border-stone-500 bg-stone-50 text-stone-500"
  };

  return (
    <p className={`min-w-0 w-full p-4 border rounded-xl text-xl font-bold ${styles[type]}`}>
      {message}
    </p>
  );
}

===== app/components/atoms/page/PaginationSelector.tsx =====
import { FiChevronLeft, FiChevronRight } from "react-icons/fi";

export interface PaginationSelectorProps {
  label: string,
  page: number;
  totalPages: number;
  size: number,
  sizeOptions: number[],
  nextPage: () => void;
  prevPage: () => void;
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
}

export function PaginationSelector({
  label,
  page,
  totalPages,
  size,
  sizeOptions,
  prevPage,
  nextPage,
  onChange
}: PaginationSelectorProps) {
  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-3 w-full">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-start gap-2">
        <label htmlFor="pageSize" className="text-sm text-slate-900">
          {label}
        </label>

        <select
          id="pageSize"
          value={size}
          onChange={(e) => {
            onChange(e.target.value);
          }}
          className="rounded-md text-slate-900 border-gray-300 py-2"
        >
          {sizeOptions.map((option) => (
            <option value={option} className="text-slate-900 text-sm">{option}</option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </div>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={prevPage}
          disabled={page === 0}
          className="text-slate-900 p-2 disabled:opacity-40"
        >
          <FiChevronLeft />
        </button>

        <span className="text-slate-900">
          Página {totalPages === 0 ? 0 : page + 1} de {totalPages}
        </span>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={nextPage}
          disabled={totalPages === 0 || page >= totalPages - 1}
          className="text-slate-900 p-2 disabled:opacity-40"
          aria-label="Próxima página"
        >
          <FiChevronRight />
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== app/components/atoms/dashboard/KpiCard.tsx =====
import clsx from "clsx";

export interface KpiCardProps {
  label: string;
  value: string;
  /** Texto pequeno abaixo do valor (ex.: variação). */
  detail?: string;
  tone?: "default" | "good" | "bad" | "muted";
}

const toneStyles = {
  default: "text-slate-500",
  good: "text-green-700",
  bad: "text-red-700",
  muted: "text-slate-500",
};

export function KpiCard({ label, value, detail, tone = "default" }: KpiCardProps) {
  return (
    <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-col gap-1 rounded-2xl bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
      <span className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
        {label}
      </span>
      <span className="text-4xl font-bold tabular-nums text-slate-900">
        {value}
      </span>
      {detail != null && (
        <span className={clsx("text-sm", toneStyles[tone])}>{detail}</span>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import clsx from "clsx";
import { dashboardPeriods, type DashboardPeriod } from "~/types/dashboard";

export interface PeriodSelectorProps {
  value: DashboardPeriod;
  onChange: (value: DashboardPeriod) => void;
}

export function PeriodSelector({ value, onChange }: PeriodSelectorProps) {
  return (
    <div
      role="group"
      aria-label="Período"
      className="inline-flex overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white"
    >
      {dashboardPeriods.map((period) => (
        <button
          key={period}
          type="button"
          aria-pressed={value === period}
          onClick={() => onChange(period)}
          className={clsx(
            "cursor-pointer px-4 py-3 text-sm transition-colors",
            value === period
              ? "bg-main text-white"
              : "text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100",
          )}
        >
          {period} dias
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}
